CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS personal_bookkeeping DEFAULT CHARACTER SET utf8mb4;
USE personal_bookkeeping;

-- 用户表
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user (
  id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  username VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  email VARCHAR(100),
  created_time DATETIME
);

-- 分类表 (预置一些数据)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS category (
  id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT,
  name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  type INT NOT NULL COMMENT '1:收入 2:支出'
);
INSERT INTO category (user_id, name, type) VALUES (0, '工资', 1), (0, '奖金', 1), (0, '餐饮', 2), (0, '交通', 2), (0, '购物', 2), (0, '住房', 2);

-- 账单表
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bill (
  id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
  amount DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
  type INT NOT NULL,
  category_id BIGINT,
  bill_date DATE,
  remark VARCHAR(255)
);

-- 预算表
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS budget (
  id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
  month VARCHAR(7),
  total_budget DECIMAL(10,2),
  used_amount DECIMAL(10,2)
);

-- 财务目标表
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS financial_goal (
  id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
  target_amount DECIMAL(10,2),
  target_date DATE,
  created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

const ICON_MAP = {

// Utilities
function pad(n) {
    return n < 10 ? '0' + n : '' + n;
}

function formatDay(d) {
    return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`;
}

function normalizeDateKey(raw) {
    if (!raw) return raw;
    // take only date portion before T
    const datePart = (raw + '').split('T')[0];
    const parts = datePart.split('-');
    if (parts.length >= 3) {
        const y = parts[0];
        const mo = pad(Number(parts[1]));
        const d = pad(Number(parts[2]));
        return `${y}-${mo}-${d}`;
    }
    // fallback: try Date parsing
    const dt = new Date(datePart);
    if (isNaN(dt)) return datePart;
    return formatDay(dt);
}

// --- NEW: read current filter values from DOM ---
function getCalendarFilters() {
    let type = '';
    let categoryId = '';
    try {
        const tEl = document.getElementById('typeFilter');
        const cEl = document.getElementById('catFilter');
        if (tEl) type = tEl.value || '';
        if (cEl) categoryId = cEl.value || '';
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn('[calendar.js] getCalendarFilters failed', e);
    }
    return {type, categoryId, viewMode: currentViewMode};
}

function monthRangeFor(date) {
    const y = date.getFullYear(), m = date.getMonth();
    const start = new Date(y, m, 1);
    const end = new Date(y, m + 1, 0); // last day
    return {start, end};
}

// Render calendar container (header + grid)
function renderCalendar() {
    try {
        const el = document.getElementById('calendar');
        if (!el) {
            console.warn('[calendar.js] calendar container #calendar not found');
            return;
        }
        el.innerHTML = '';

        // header
        const header = document.createElement('div');
        header.className = 'd-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-2';
        const title = document.createElement('div');
        title.className = 'fw-bold';
        title.id = 'calendarTitle';
        const nav = document.createElement('div');
        nav.innerHTML = `
            <div class="btn-group btn-group-sm" role="group">
                <button class="btn btn-outline-secondary" id="calPrev">‹</button>
                <button class="btn btn-outline-secondary" id="calToday">今天</button>
                <button class="btn btn-outline-secondary" id="calNext">›</button>
            </div>
        `;
        header.appendChild(title);
        header.appendChild(nav);
        el.appendChild(header);

        document.getElementById('calPrev')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
            currentDate.setMonth(currentDate.getMonth() - 1);
            refreshAll();
        });
        document.getElementById('calNext')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
            currentDate.setMonth(currentDate.getMonth() + 1);
            refreshAll();
        });
        document.getElementById('calToday')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
            currentDate = new Date();
            refreshAll();
        });

        // weekday header
        const weekNames = ['日', '一', '二', '三', '四', '五', '六'];
        const wk = document.createElement('div');
        wk.className = 'd-flex';
        weekNames.forEach(n => {
            const c = document.createElement('div');
            c.style.flex = '1';
            c.style.padding = '8px 6px';
            c.className = 'cal-weekday';
            c.innerText = n;
            wk.appendChild(c);
        });
        el.appendChild(wk);

        // grid
        const grid = document.createElement('div');
        grid.id = 'calGrid';
        grid.style.display = 'grid';
        grid.style.gridTemplateColumns = 'repeat(7, 1fr)';
        grid.style.gap = '10px';
        grid.style.marginTop = '8px';
        el.appendChild(grid);

        populateDays();
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('[calendar.js] renderCalendar failed', err);
    }
}

function populateDays() {
    const grid = document.getElementById('calGrid');
    if (!grid) return;
    grid.innerHTML = '';
    const {start, end} = monthRangeFor(currentDate);
    const firstWeekDay = start.getDay();
    const totalDays = end.getDate();

    // fill leading blanks (empty placeholders)
    for (let i = 0; i < firstWeekDay; i++) {
        const cell = document.createElement('div');
        cell.className = 'p-2';
        cell.style.minHeight = '90px';
        cell.style.background = 'transparent';
        grid.appendChild(cell);
    }

    for (let d = 1; d <= totalDays; d++) {
        const dateObj = new Date(start.getFullYear(), start.getMonth(), d);
        const dateStr = formatDay(dateObj);

        const cell = document.createElement('div');
        cell.className = 'p-2 fc-day';
        cell.style.minHeight = '110px';
        // center content
        cell.style.display = 'flex';
        cell.style.flexDirection = 'column';
        cell.style.justifyContent = 'center';
        cell.style.alignItems = 'center';
        cell.style.background = '#ebebeb';
        cell.style.borderRadius = '15px';
        cell.style.cursor = 'pointer';
        cell.dataset.date = dateStr;

        // day meta
        const meta = document.createElement('div');
        meta.className = 'day-meta';
        meta.style.display = 'flex';
        meta.style.flexDirection = 'column';
        meta.style.alignItems = 'center';
        meta.style.gap = '6px';
        const dateLabel = document.createElement('div');
        dateLabel.className = 'date-label';
        dateLabel.style.fontSize = '1.15rem';
        dateLabel.style.lineHeight = '1';
        dateLabel.innerText = d;
        const amt = document.createElement('div');
        amt.className = 'day-amount';
        amt.innerText = '￥0.00';
        meta.appendChild(dateLabel);
        meta.appendChild(amt);
        cell.appendChild(meta);

        // events container
        const eventsWrap = document.createElement('div');
        eventsWrap.className = 'events small mt-2';
        cell.appendChild(eventsWrap);

        // click to open drawer + mark selection
        cell.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
            if (e.target.closest('.day-event')) return;
            // clear previous selection
            document.querySelectorAll('.fc-day.selected').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('selected'));
            cell.classList.add('selected');
            openDrawer(dateStr);
        });

        grid.appendChild(cell);
    }

    // after building days, fetch events & heatmap
    const startStr = formatDay(start);
    const endStr = formatDay(end);
    fetchHeatMap(startStr, endStr);
    fetchEvents(startStr, endStr);

    // update title
    const titleEl = document.getElementById('calendarTitle');
    const y = currentDate.getFullYear(), m = currentDate.getMonth() + 1;
    titleEl.innerText = `${y} 年 ${m} 月`;
}

// --- modified: include filters and trigger UI update after fetching events ---
function fetchEvents(startStr, endStr) {
    const {type, categoryId} = getCalendarFilters();
    let url = `/api/calendar/events?start=${startStr}&end=${endStr}`;
    if (type) url += `&type=${encodeURIComponent(type)}`;
    if (categoryId) url += `&categoryId=${encodeURIComponent(categoryId)}`;

    // Fetch events for the month and cache them by date. We intentionally do not render
    // event items inside calendar cells (the drawer will load full details on click).
    fetch(url).then(r => r.json()).then(data => {
        eventsByDate = {};
        if (!Array.isArray(data)) return;
        data.forEach(ev => {
            const raw = ev.start ? (ev.start.split('T')[0]) : ev.date || ev.startDate;
            const st = normalizeDateKey(raw);
            if (!st) return;
            if (!eventsByDate[st]) eventsByDate[st] = [];
            eventsByDate[st].push(ev);
        });
        // Update calendar cells from event cache (if heatMapData is not present for a date,
        // this provides a responsive per-cell amount summary).
        try {
            updateCellsFromEvents();
            // also re-apply heatmap styles so any existing heatMapData still wins
            applyHeatMapStyles();
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn('[calendar.js] post-fetch update failed', e);
        }
    }).catch(() => {
    });
}

// --- modified: refetch both events and heatmap (keeps previous behavior) ---
function refetchEvents() {
    const {start, end} = monthRangeFor(currentDate);
    const s = formatDay(start), e = formatDay(end);
    fetchEvents(s, e);
    fetchHeatMap(s, e);
}

// --- modified: changeViewMode should trigger a refetch to update heatmap + cells ---
function changeViewMode(mode) {
    currentViewMode = mode;
    // update visuals immediately
    applyHeatMapStyles();
    // refetch server-side heatmap if server supports viewMode filtering
    refetchEvents();
}

// --- modified: include filters when fetching heatmap so heatmap reflects left controls ---
function fetchHeatMap(start, end) {
    const {type, categoryId, viewMode} = getCalendarFilters();
    let url = `/api/calendar/heatmap?start=${start}&end=${end}`;
    if (type) url += `&type=${encodeURIComponent(type)}`;
    if (categoryId) url += `&categoryId=${encodeURIComponent(categoryId)}`;
    // include viewMode as hint for server-side aggregation (optional)
    if (viewMode) url += `&viewMode=${encodeURIComponent(viewMode)}`;

    fetch(url)
        .then(r => r.json()).then(data => {
        heatMapData = data || {};
        applyHeatMapStyles();
    })
        .catch(() => {
            heatMapData = {};
            applyHeatMapStyles();
        });
}

function interpColor(hex1, hex2, t) {
    // 使用对数曲线偏移 t，使小值更靠近 0（浅色更多），映射保持在 [0,1]
    const biased = (t <= 0) ? 0 : Math.log1p(9 * t) / Math.log1p(9); // concave, 0..1 -> 0..1
    const n1 = parseInt(hex1.slice(1), 16), n2 = parseInt(hex2.slice(1), 16);
    const r1 = (n1 >> 16) & 255, g1 = (n1 >> 8) & 255, b1 = n1 & 255;
    const r2 = (n2 >> 16) & 255, g2 = (n2 >> 8) & 255, b2 = n2 & 255;
    const r = Math.round(r1 + (r2 - r1) * biased), g = Math.round(g1 + (g2 - g1) * biased),
        b = Math.round(b1 + (b2 - b1) * biased);
    return '#' + ((1 << 24) + (r << 16) + (g << 8) + b).toString(16).slice(1);
}

function applyHeatMapStyles() {
    // clear
    document.querySelectorAll('#calGrid [data-date]').forEach(cell => {
        cell.style.backgroundColor = '';
        const existing = cell.querySelector('.balance-label');
        if (existing) existing.remove();
        const amtEl = cell.querySelector('.day-amount');
        if (amtEl) {
            amtEl.innerText = '0.00';
            amtEl.style.color = '#8b8f8f';
        }
    });

    // color ranges (light -> deep)
    // Choose light colors near-white for small amounts, deep saturated colors for large amounts
    const redLight = '#fff5f6'; // near-white with tiny red tint for small expenses
    const redDeep = '#fc9aa5';  // saturated red for large expenses
    const greenLight = '#f3fff6'; // near-white with tiny green tint for small incomes
    const greenDeep = '#90dbba'; // saturated green for large incomes

    // Dynamically compute the largest absolute amount in the current heatMapData
    let maxAbs = 0;
    Object.keys(heatMapData || {}).forEach(d => {
        const day = heatMapData[d];
        if (!day) return;
        const inc = parseFloat(day.income || 0);
        const exp = parseFloat(day.expense || 0);
        let a = null;
        if (day.balance != null) a = parseFloat(day.balance);
        else a = inc - exp;
        if (Number.isFinite(a)) maxAbs = Math.max(maxAbs, Math.abs(a));
    });
    const MAX_SCALE = Math.max(1, maxAbs);

    Object.keys(heatMapData).forEach(date => {
        const dayData = heatMapData[date];
        const cel = document.querySelector(`#calGrid [data-date='${date}']`);
        if (!cel || !dayData) return;
        const income = parseFloat(dayData.income || 0);
        const expense = parseFloat(dayData.expense || 0);
        // prefer explicit balance if provided
        let amount = null;
        if (dayData.balance != null) amount = parseFloat(dayData.balance);
        else amount = income - expense;

        // don't override selected cell's background
        if (cel.classList.contains('selected')) {
            const amtEl = cel.querySelector('.day-amount');
            if (amtEl) {
                const val = Number(amount || 0);
                const sign = val > 0 ? '+' : (val < 0 ? '-' : '');
                amtEl.innerText = (sign ? sign : '') + Math.abs(val).toFixed(2);
                amtEl.style.color = val > 0 ? '#3db587' : (val < 0 ? '#dd606e' : '#8b8f8f');
            }
            return;
        }

        // default background
        cel.style.background = '#ebebeb';

        if (amount == null || amount === 0) {
            const amtEl = cel.querySelector('.day-amount');
            if (amtEl) {
                amtEl.innerText = '0.00';
                amtEl.style.color = '#8b8f8f';
            }
            return;
        }

        const absv = Math.min(1, Math.abs(amount) / MAX_SCALE);
        const t = absv;
        let bg, amtText, color;
        if (amount > 0) {
            bg = interpColor(greenLight, greenDeep, t);
            amtText = '+ ' + Math.abs(amount).toFixed(2);
            color = '#3db587';
        } else {
            bg = interpColor(redLight, redDeep, t);
            amtText = '- ' + Math.abs(amount).toFixed(2);
            color = '#dd606e';
        }
        cel.style.background = bg;
        const amtEl = cel.querySelector('.day-amount');
        if (amtEl) {
            amtEl.innerText = amtText;
            amtEl.style.color = color;
        }
    });
}

function refreshAll() {
    renderCalendar();
}

function openDrawer(dateStr) {
    const body = document.getElementById('drawerBody');
    const headerDate = document.getElementById('drawerDate');
    const headerTotal = document.getElementById('drawerTotal');
    headerDate.innerText = dateStr;
    headerTotal.innerText = '合计：￥0.00';
    body.innerHTML = '<div class="text-muted small">加载中...</div>';

    // Prefer cached monthly events if available to avoid an extra request
    const cached = eventsByDate && eventsByDate[dateStr];
    if (Array.isArray(cached) && cached.length > 0) {
        // ensure cached items look like transactions (have amount or category/title)
        const looksLikeTx = cached.every(ev => ev && (ev.amount != null || ev.category || ev.title));
        if (looksLikeTx) {
            renderDayDetails(dateStr, cached);
            return;
        }
        // otherwise fall through to fetch full day details
    }

    // include filters in day fetch so drawer displays filtered transactions
    const {type, categoryId} = getCalendarFilters();
    let dayUrl = `/api/calendar/day?date=${dateStr}`;
    if (type) dayUrl += `&type=${encodeURIComponent(type)}`;
    if (categoryId) dayUrl += `&categoryId=${encodeURIComponent(categoryId)}`;

    fetch(dayUrl).then(r => r.json()).then(data => {
        renderDayDetails(dateStr, data);
    }).catch(() => {
        const sample = [{time: '09:20', category: '餐饮', remark: '早餐', amount: -12.5}, {
            time: '12:10',
            category: '工资',
            remark: '报销',
            amount: +200
        }];
        renderDayDetails(dateStr, sample);
    });
}

// --- NEW: update cell amounts from events cache when heatMapData not available ---
function updateCellsFromEvents() {
    // iterate over date cells and set day-amount based on eventsByDate
    document.querySelectorAll('#calGrid [data-date]').forEach(cell => {
        const date = cell.dataset.date;
        const amtEl = cell.querySelector('.day-amount');
        // if heatMapData contains this date, prefer heatmap rendering
        if (heatMapData && heatMapData[date]) return;
        let amt = 0;
        let found = false;
        const list = eventsByDate && eventsByDate[date];
        if (Array.isArray(list)) {
            list.forEach(ev => {
                let val = null;
                if (ev.amount !== undefined && ev.amount !== null) val = Number(ev.amount);
                else if (ev.amt !== undefined && ev.amt !== null) val = Number(ev.amt);
                else if (ev.value !== undefined && ev.value !== null) val = Number(ev.value);
                // try negative/positive signs in title/category as fallback (best-effort)
                if (!Number.isFinite(val) && ev.title) {
                    const m = (ev.title + '').match(/([+-]?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/);
                    if (m) val = Number(m[1]);
                }
                if (Number.isFinite(val)) {
                    amt += val;
                    found = true;
                }
            });
        }
        if (amtEl) {
            if (!found || amt === 0) {
                amtEl.innerText = '0.00';
                amtEl.style.color = '#8b8f8f';
            } else {
                const sign = amt > 0 ? '+ ' : '- ';
                amtEl.innerText = sign + Math.abs(amt).toFixed(2);
                amtEl.style.color = amt > 0 ? '#3db587' : '#dd606e';
            }
        }
    });
}

// --- Accept different response shapes
function renderDayDetails(dateStr, list) {
    const body = document.getElementById('drawerBody');
    const headerTotal = document.getElementById('drawerTotal');

    // Accept different response shapes
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) {
        if (list && Array.isArray(list.records)) list = list.records;
        else if (list && Array.isArray(list.data)) list = list.data;
        else list = [];
    }

    if (list.length === 0) {
        body.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-muted small py-3">当天无流水</div>';
        headerTotal.innerText = '合计：￥0.00';
        return;
    }

    let total = 0;
    body.innerHTML = '';

    list.forEach(rawTx => {
        const tx = rawTx || {};
        // raw category/title may contain amount; prefer explicit numeric fields
        let rawCategory = (tx.category || tx.title || '') + '';
        rawCategory = rawCategory.trim();

        // time/remark
        const time = (tx.time || tx.t || '') + '';
        const remark = (tx.remark || tx.note || '') + '';

        // try explicit numeric amount fields first
        let amountVal = null;
        if (tx.amount !== undefined && tx.amount !== null) amountVal = Number(tx.amount);
        else if (tx.amt !== undefined && tx.amt !== null) amountVal = Number(tx.amt);
        else if (tx.value !== undefined && tx.value !== null) amountVal = Number(tx.value);

        let hasNumeric = Number.isFinite(amountVal);

        // if not found, try to extract trailing amount from rawCategory
        let cleanedType = rawCategory;
        if (!hasNumeric && rawCategory) {
            // match patterns like "[住房] -166.00", "餐饮 -12.5", "住房+200" etc.
            const m = rawCategory.match(/^(.*?)\s*[￥¥]?\s*([+-]?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)(?:\s*$)/);
            if (m) {
                cleanedType = (m[1] || '').trim();
                const parsed = Number(m[2]);
                if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
                    amountVal = parsed;
                    hasNumeric = true;
                }
            }
        }

        // final typeText: strip surrounding brackets if present
        let typeText = (cleanedType || '').replace(/^\s*\[(.*)\]\s*$/, '$1').trim();
        if (!typeText && rawCategory) {
            // if cleaning removed everything, fall back to original without numbers
            typeText = rawCategory.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/[+-]?\d+(?:\.\d+)?/, '').trim();
        }

        // format amount text
        let amountText = '';
        if (hasNumeric) {
            amountText = (amountVal >= 0 ? '+' : '-') + Math.abs(amountVal).toFixed(2);
        } else {
            amountText = tx.amountText || '';
        }

        // build DOM
        const item = document.createElement('div');
        item.className = 'tx-item' + (hasNumeric ? (amountVal >= 0 ? ' positive' : ' negative') : '');

        const left = document.createElement('div');
        left.className = 'left';
        // determine icon class by id or name; fall back to arrow/circle
        let iconClass = '';
        const catId = tx.categoryId || tx.catId || tx.cid || (tx.category && (/^\d+$/.test(tx.category) ? tx.category : null));
        if (catId && ICON_MAP[String(catId)]) iconClass = ICON_MAP[String(catId)];
        else {
            const nm = (typeText || cleanedType || rawCategory || '').toString();
            for (const k in NAME_MAP) {
                if (k && nm.indexOf(k) !== -1) {
                    iconClass = NAME_MAP[k];
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        if (!iconClass) {
            if (hasNumeric) iconClass = amountVal >= 0 ? 'fa-arrow-up' : 'fa-arrow-down';
            else iconClass = 'fa-circle';
        }
        const iconHtml = `<i class="fas ${iconClass}"></i>`;
        left.innerHTML = `<div class="avatar">${iconHtml}</div><div class="meta"><div class="type">${typeText}</div><div class="time-remark">${time} ${remark}</div></div>`;

        const right = document.createElement('div');
        right.className = 'amount';
        right.innerHTML = `<div class="fw-bold">${amountText || ''}</div>`;

        item.appendChild(left);
        item.appendChild(right);
        body.appendChild(item);

        if (hasNumeric) {
            total += Number(amountVal);
        }
    });

    headerTotal.innerText = '合计：' + (total >= 0 ? '+' : '') + '￥' + Math.abs(total).toFixed(2);
}

// initial boot
if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', renderCalendar);
else renderCalendar();

<script>
    fetch('/api/insight/goal')
        .then(res => res.json())
        .then(data => {
            document.getElementById('saved').innerText = '￥' + data.currentSaved;

            document.getElementById('date').innerText = data.estimateDate;

            const el = document.getElementById('progress');
            el.style.width = p;
            el.innerText = p;
        });

    function toggleForm() {
        const form = document.getElementById('goal-form');
        form.style.display = form.style.display === 'none' ? 'block' : 'none';
    }

    function saveGoal() {
        const amount = document.getElementById('new-amount').value;
        const date = document.getElementById('new-date').value;

        if (!amount || !date) {
            alert("请填写完整！");
            return;
        }

        fetch('/api/insight/goal', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({amount, date})
        }).then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.success) {
                    alert("设置成功！");
                    location.reload();
                }
            });
    }
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
    fetch('/api/insight/profile')
        .then(res => res.json())
        .then(data => {
            document.getElementById('score').innerText = data.score || '?';
            document.getElementById('type').innerText = data.type || '未知类型';

            const rate = data.savingsRate || '0.0%';
            const rateVal = parseFloat(rate.replace('%', ''));
            const bar = document.getElementById('saving-bar');

            bar.style.width = Math.min(rateVal < 0 ? 0 : rateVal, 100) + '%';
            bar.innerText = rate;

            if (rateVal < 20) bar.style.background = '#f56c6c';
            else if (rateVal < 40) bar.style.background = '#e6a23c';

            document.getElementById('saving-text').innerHTML = `您的储蓄率：<strong>${rate}</strong><br>建议保持在 20% 以上。`;
        });
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
    fetch('/api/insight/risk')
        .then(res => res.json())
        .then(alerts => {
            const container = document.getElementById('alert-container');
            if (!alerts || alerts.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align: center; color: #67C23A; margin-top: 50px; font-size: 20px;">✅ 当前财务状况健康，无异常风险。</div>';
            } else {
                container.innerHTML = alerts.map(alert => `
                        <div class="alert-box ${alert.level}">
                            <div style="font-weight: bold; margin-bottom: 5px;">${alert.title}</div>
                            <div>${alert.desc}</div>
                        </div>
                    `).join('');
            }
        });
</script>

<script>
    fetch('/api/insight/timeline')
        .then(res => res.json())
        .then(list => {
            const box = document.getElementById('timeline-box');
            if (!list || list.length === 0) {
                box.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#999">暂无数据</div>';
                return;
            }
            box.innerHTML = list.map(item => `
                    <div class="timeline-item">
                        <div class="timeline-dot ${item.isKeyInfo ? 'key' : ''}"></div>
                        <div style="font-size:13px;color:#909399;margin-bottom:5px;">${item.date}</div>
                        <div class="card ${item.isKeyInfo ? 'key-event' : ''}">
                            <span class="amount">￥${item.amount}</span>
                            <div style="font-weight:bold;">${item.category} ${item.isKeyInfo ? '<span style="color:red;font-size:12px">[大额]</span>' : ''}</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `).join('');
        });
</script>
</body>

<script th:inline="javascript">
    // ✅ 推荐写法：
    // ✅ 修复版：使用 JSON.parse 解析字符串
    // 如果后端传的是 "{}" 字符串，解析后就是空对象，不会报错
    const analysisData = JSON.parse(/*[[${dataJson}]]*/ '{}');
    const enhancedData = JSON.parse(/*[[${enhancedDataJson}]]*/ '{}');


    console.log("Loaded Data:", analysisData);
    console.log("Loaded Enhanced:", enhancedData);

    // --- 1. 月度趋势图 ---
    const trendChart = echarts.init(document.getElementById('trendChart'));
    if (analysisData && analysisData.monthly) {
        const months = Object.keys(analysisData.monthly);
        const incomeData = months.map(m => analysisData.monthly[m][0]);
        const expenseData = months.map(m => analysisData.monthly[m][1]);

        trendChart.setOption({
            tooltip: {trigger: 'axis'},
            legend: {data: ['收入', '支出'], top: '0%', left: 'center', padding: 10},
            grid: {left: '3%', right: '4%', bottom: '3%', top: '12%', containLabel: true},
            xAxis: {type: 'category', boundaryGap: false, data: months},
            yAxis: {type: 'value'},
            series: [
                {
                    name: '收入',
                    type: 'line',
                    smooth: true,
                    data: incomeData,
                    itemStyle: {color: '#198754'},
                    areaStyle: {opacity: 0.1}
                },
                {
                    name: '支出',
                    type: 'line',
                    smooth: true,
                    data: expenseData,
                    itemStyle: {color: '#dc3545'},
                    areaStyle: {opacity: 0.1}
                }
            ]
        });
    }

    // --- 日度趋势图 ---
    const dailyChart = echarts.init(document.getElementById('dailyChart'));
    if (analysisData && analysisData.daily) {
        const days = Object.keys(analysisData.daily);
        const dailyIncome = days.map(d => analysisData.daily[d][0]);
        const dailyExpense = days.map(d => analysisData.daily[d][1]);

        dailyChart.setOption({
            tooltip: {trigger: 'axis'},
            legend: {data: ['收入', '支出', '收入趋势', '支出趋势'], top: '0%'},
            grid: {left: '3%', right: '4%', bottom: '15%', top: '12%', containLabel: true},
            xAxis: {type: 'category', data: days},
            yAxis: {type: 'value'},
            dataZoom: [{type: 'slider', show: true, xAxisIndex: [0], start: 0, end: 100, bottom: 10}],
            series: [
                {
                    name: '收入',
                    type: 'bar',
                    barMaxWidth: 30,
                    data: dailyIncome,
                    itemStyle: {color: '#198754', borderRadius: [3, 3, 0, 0]}
                },
                {
                    name: '支出',
                    type: 'bar',
                    barMaxWidth: 30,
                    data: dailyExpense,
                    itemStyle: {color: '#dc3545', borderRadius: [3, 3, 0, 0]}
                },
                {name: '收入趋势', type: 'line', smooth: true, data: dailyIncome, itemStyle: {color: '#20c997'}},
                {name: '支出趋势', type: 'line', smooth: true, data: dailyExpense, itemStyle: {color: '#ff6b6b'}}
            ]
        });
    }

    // --- 2. 支出饼图 ---
    const expensePieChart = echarts.init(document.getElementById('expensePieChart'));
    if (analysisData && analysisData.expensePie) {
        const expensePieData = Object.entries(analysisData.expensePie).map(([name, value]) => ({name, value}));
        expensePieChart.setOption({
            tooltip: {trigger: 'item', formatter: '{b}: {c} ({d}%)'},
            legend: {bottom: '0%'},
            series: [{
                type: 'pie',
                radius: ['40%', '70%'],
                itemStyle: {borderRadius: 10, borderColor: '#fff', borderWidth: 2},
                data: expensePieData.length ? expensePieData : [{name: '无数据', value: 0}]
            }]
        });
    }

    // --- 3. 收入饼图 ---
    const incomePieChart = echarts.init(document.getElementById('incomePieChart'));
    if (analysisData && analysisData.incomePie) {
        const incomePieData = Object.entries(analysisData.incomePie).map(([name, value]) => ({name, value}));
        incomePieChart.setOption({
            tooltip: {trigger: 'item', formatter: '{b}: {c} ({d}%)'},
            legend: {bottom: '0%'},
            series: [{
                type: 'pie',
                radius: '50%',
                data: incomePieData.length ? incomePieData : [{name: '无数据', value: 0}],
                color: ['#91cc75', '#5470c6', '#fac858', '#ee6666', '#73c0de']
            }]
        });
    }

    // --- 📊 4. [新增] 支出 Top 5 ---
    if (enhancedData && enhancedData.top5) {
        const top5Chart = echarts.init(document.getElementById('top5Chart'));
        const t5 = enhancedData.top5;
        top5Chart.setOption({
            grid: {left: '3%', right: '10%', bottom: '3%', containLabel: true},
            xAxis: {type: 'value'},
            yAxis: {type: 'category', data: t5.map(i => i.category), inverse: true}, // inverse让第一名在上面
            tooltip: {trigger: 'axis'},
            series: [{
                type: 'bar',
                data: t5.map(i => i.amount),
                itemStyle: {color: '#fd7e14'},
                label: {show: true, position: 'right'}
            }]
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => top5Chart.resize());
    }

    // --- 📊 5. [新增] 同比/环比 ---
    if (enhancedData && enhancedData.yoy) {
        const yoyChart = echarts.init(document.getElementById('yoyChart'));
        const yoy = enhancedData.yoy;
        yoyChart.setOption({
            tooltip: {trigger: 'axis', axisPointer: {type: 'shadow'}},
            legend: {data: ['本期', '上期']},
            xAxis: {type: 'category', data: ['收入', '支出']},
            yAxis: {type: 'value'},
            series: [
                {
                    name: '本期',
                    type: 'bar',
                    data: [yoy.current.income, yoy.current.expense],
                    itemStyle: {color: '#0d6efd'}
                },
                {
                    name: '上期',
                    type: 'bar',
                    data: [yoy.previous.income, yoy.previous.expense],
                    itemStyle: {color: '#adb5bd'}
                }
            ]
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => yoyChart.resize());
    }

    // --- 📊 6. [新增] 消费频率 ---
    if (enhancedData && enhancedData.freq) {
        const freqChart = echarts.init(document.getElementById('freqChart'));
        freqChart.setOption({
            xAxis: {type: 'category', data: enhancedData.freq.map(i => i.category), axisLabel: {rotate: 45}},
            yAxis: {type: 'value'},
            tooltip: {trigger: 'axis'},
            series: [{type: 'bar', data: enhancedData.freq.map(i => i.count), itemStyle: {color: '#6610f2'}}]
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => freqChart.resize());
    }

    // --- 📊 7. [新增] 星期消费 ---
    if (enhancedData && enhancedData.weekly) {
        const weeklyChart = echarts.init(document.getElementById('weeklyChart'));
        const wDays = Object.keys(enhancedData.weekly);
        const wVals = Object.values(enhancedData.weekly);
        weeklyChart.setOption({
            xAxis: {type: 'category', data: wDays},
            yAxis: {type: 'value'},
            tooltip: {trigger: 'axis'},
            series: [{type: 'bar', data: wVals, itemStyle: {color: '#20c997'}}]
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => weeklyChart.resize());
    }

    // --- 📊 8. [新增] 预算趋势 ---
    if (enhancedData && enhancedData.budgetTrend) {
        const btChart = echarts.init(document.getElementById('budgetTrendChart'));
        const trend = enhancedData.budgetTrend;
        const budgetLine = trend.dailyUsed.map(d => trend.budget);
        const usedLine = trend.dailyUsed.map(d => d.used);
        const dates = trend.dailyUsed.map(d => d.date);

        btChart.setOption({
            tooltip: {trigger: 'axis'},
            legend: {data: ['预算限额', '累积支出']},
            xAxis: {type: 'category', data: dates},
            yAxis: {type: 'value'},
            series: [
                {
                    name: '预算限额',
                    type: 'line',
                    data: budgetLine,
                    lineStyle: {type: 'dashed', color: 'red'},
                    symbol: 'none'
                },
                {
                    name: '累积支出',
                    type: 'line',
                    data: usedLine,
                    areaStyle: {opacity: 0.2},
                    itemStyle: {color: '#0d6efd'}
                }
            ]
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => btChart.resize());
    }

    // --- 📊 9. [新增] 预算风险 ---
    if (enhancedData && enhancedData.budgetRisk) {
        const riskChart = echarts.init(document.getElementById('budgetRiskChart'));
        const rate = enhancedData.budgetRisk.rate || 0;
        riskChart.setOption({
            series: [{
                type: 'gauge',
                progress: {show: true},
                detail: {valueAnimation: true, formatter: '{value}%', fontSize: 20},
                data: [{value: rate, name: '消耗率'}],
                axisLine: {lineStyle: {color: [ [0.6, '#91cc75'], [0.9, '#fac858'], [1, '#ff6b6b'] ]}}
            }]
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => riskChart.resize());
    }

    // --- 📊 10. [新增] 健康雷达 ---
    if (enhancedData && enhancedData.health) {
        const hChart = echarts.init(document.getElementById('healthChart'));
        const h = enhancedData.health;
        hChart.setOption({
            tooltip: {},
            radar: {
                indicator: [
                    {name: '储蓄率', max: 1},
                    {name: '收支平衡', max: 1},
                    {name: '支出结构健康度', max: 1},
                    {name: '消费稳定性', max: 1}
                ]
            },
            series: [{
                type: 'radar',
                itemStyle: {color: '#0dcaf0'},
                areaStyle: {opacity: 0.2},
                data: [{
                    value: [
                        h.savingRate,
                        h.balance,
                        (1 - (h.expenseFocus || 0)),
                        h.stability
                    ],
                    name: '财务健康度'
                }]
            }]
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => hChart.resize());
    }

    // 窗口大小改变时重绘
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
        trendChart.resize();
        dailyChart.resize();
        expensePieChart.resize();
        incomePieChart.resize();
    });
</script>

<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
    // 进度条动画
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        const progressBars = document.querySelectorAll('.progress-bar-animated');
        progressBars.forEach(bar => {
            const width = bar.style.width;
            bar.style.width = '0';
            setTimeout(() => {
                bar.style.width = width;
            }, 300);
        });
    });
</script>

<script src="/js/calendar.js"></script>
<script>
    // Debug helper to confirm template and static JS are reachable
    try {
        console.log('[calendar.html] template loaded');
        fetch('/js/calendar.js').then(r => {
            console.log('[calendar.html] /js/calendar.js status', r.status);
            return r.text();
        }).then(txt => {
            // only log small portion to avoid clogging
            console.log('[calendar.html] /js/calendar.js preview:', txt.slice(0, 120));
        }).catch(e => {
            console.warn('[calendar.html] /js/calendar.js fetch failed', e);
        });
    } catch (e) {
        console.warn('[calendar.html] debug script failed', e);
    }

    // Post-load sanity check: attempt to call renderCalendar and surface errors visibly in the page
    setTimeout(() => {
        try {
            if (typeof renderCalendar === 'function') {
                try {
                    renderCalendar();
                    console.log('[calendar.html] renderCalendar invoked from sanity check');
                } catch (e) {
                    console.error('[calendar.html] renderCalendar threw', e);
                    const el = document.getElementById('calendar');
                    if (el) el.innerHTML = `<div class="alert alert-danger p-3">日历初始化失败：${(e && e.message) || e}</div>`;
                }
            } else {
                console.warn('[calendar.html] renderCalendar not defined');
                const el = document.getElementById('calendar');
                if (el) el.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-muted p-3">日历脚本未加载或初始化失败，请查看控制台</div>';
            }

            const cal = document.getElementById('calendar');
            if (cal && cal.children.length === 0) {
                cal.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-muted p-3">日历未能加载（无内容），请在控制台查看错误信息。</div>';
            }
        } catch (err) {
            console.error('[calendar.html] sanity check failed', err);
            const el = document.getElementById('calendar');
            if (el) el.innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-danger p-3">日历初始化检测遇到错误，请查看控制台</div>';
        }
    }, 120);

    // --- Sync drawer max-height to left calendar height ---
    (function () {
        const drawer = document.getElementById('dayDrawer');
        const calendarMain = document.querySelector('.calendar-main');
        if (!drawer || !calendarMain) return;

        function setDrawerMaxHeight() {
            try {
                // measure calendar-main height (content box)
                const rect = calendarMain.getBoundingClientRect();
                // if height is zero (not rendered yet), skip
                if (!rect || rect.height <= 0) return;
                drawer.style.maxHeight = Math.round(rect.height) + 'px';
            } catch (e) {
                console.warn('[calendar.html] setDrawerMaxHeight failed', e);
            }
        }

        // debounce helper
        function debounce(fn, wait) {
            let t = null;
            return function () {
                clearTimeout(t);
                t = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, arguments), wait);
            };
        }

        const debouncedSet = debounce(setDrawerMaxHeight, 120);

        // run once after short delay to allow initial layout
        setTimeout(setDrawerMaxHeight, 200);
        setTimeout(setDrawerMaxHeight, 800);

        // run on resize
        window.addEventListener('resize', debouncedSet);

        // observe calendar content changes (e.g., when calendar is rendered or refreshed)
        const calEl = document.getElementById('calendar');
        if (calEl && window.MutationObserver) {
            const mo = new MutationObserver(debouncedSet);
            mo.observe(calEl, {childList: true, subtree: true, attributes: true});
        }

        // also observe changes to left sidebar that might affect height
        const leftCol = document.querySelector('.calendar-main');
        if (leftCol && window.MutationObserver) {
            const mo2 = new MutationObserver(debouncedSet);
            mo2.observe(leftCol, {childList: true, subtree: true, attributes: true});
        }

        // expose for debugging
        window._syncDrawerHeight = setDrawerMaxHeight;
    })();
</script>
</body>
</html>

                title="上传文件">
            <i class="fas fa-paperclip"></i>
        </button>
        <input class="form-control" id="chatInput" onkeypress="if(event.keyCode==13) sendMsg()" placeholder="输入问题..."
               type="text">
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="sendMsg()"><i class="fas fa-paper-plane"></i></button>
    </div>
    <div class="text-center p-1 bg-white border-top">
        <small class="text-muted" onclick="clearHistory()" style="cursor: pointer;">🗑️ 清除记忆</small>
    </div>

<!-- ============ JavaScript 逻辑 ============ -->
<script>
    const chatBody = document.getElementById('chatBody');
    const chatInput = document.getElementById('chatInput');
    const chatWindow = document.getElementById('chatWindow');

    // 👇 恢复聊天窗口状态
    (function restoreChatState() {
        const isVisible = sessionStorage.getItem('chatVisible') === 'true';
        const history = sessionStorage.getItem('chatHistory');

        if (history) {
            chatBody.innerHTML = history;
            setTimeout(scrollToBottom, 100);
        }

        if (isVisible) {
            chatWindow.style.display = 'flex';
        }

        // 清除状态，避免意外干扰下次正常进入
        sessionStorage.removeItem('chatVisible');
        sessionStorage.removeItem('chatHistory');
    })();

    // 👇 保存聊天窗口状态
    function saveChatState() {
        const isVisible = chatWindow.style.display === 'flex';
        sessionStorage.setItem('chatVisible', isVisible);
        sessionStorage.setItem('chatHistory', chatBody.innerHTML);
    }

    // 1. 开关聊天窗
    function toggleChat() {
        chatWindow.style.display = (chatWindow.style.display === 'none' || chatWindow.style.display === '') ? 'flex' : 'none';
        if (chatWindow.style.display === 'flex') chatInput.focus();
    }

    // 🟢 复用模态框逻辑
    let billModalInstance = null;

    function getBillModal() {
        if (!billModalInstance) {
            billModalInstance = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('addBillModal'));
        }
        return billModalInstance;
    }

    // 打开新增窗口
    function openAddModal() {
        document.getElementById('modalTitle').innerText = '记一笔';
        document.querySelector('#addBillModal form').reset();
        document.querySelector('[name="id"]').value = '';
        document.querySelector('[name="billDate"]').value = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
        getBillModal().show();
    }

    // 打开编辑窗口
    function editBill(id, type, amount, categoryId, date, remark) {
        document.getElementById('modalTitle').innerText = '编辑账单';
        document.querySelector('[name="id"]').value = id;
        document.querySelector('[name="type"]').value = type;
        document.querySelector('[name="amount"]').value = amount;
        document.querySelector('[name="categoryId"]').value = categoryId;
        document.querySelector('[name="billDate"]').value = date;
        document.querySelector('[name="remark"]').value = remark === 'null' ? '' : remark;
        getBillModal().show();
    }

    // 金额校验功能 - SweetAlert2 优化版
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        const billForm = document.getElementById('billForm');
        const amountInput = document.getElementById('amountInput');
        const categorySelect = billForm.querySelector('select[name="categoryId"]');

        // 表单提交事件监听器
        billForm.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault(); // 拦截默认提交

            const amount = parseFloat(amountInput.value);
            const categoryId = categorySelect ? categorySelect.value : null;

            // 1. 检查分类
            if (categorySelect && (!categoryId || categoryId === "")) {
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'info',
                    title: '提示',
                    text: '请选择账单分类',
                    confirmButtonText: '好的'
                });
                return;
            }

            // 2. 检查金额是否为负数或0
            if (isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0) {
                Swal.fire({
                    icon: 'error',
                    title: '错误',
                    text: '账单金额必须大于0！',
                    confirmButtonText: '重试'
                });
                return;
            }

            // 3. 检查金额是否超过10000
            if (amount > 10000) {
                Swal.fire({
                    title: '金额较大确认',
                    html: `您输入的金额为 <b class="text-danger">¥${amount.toFixed(2)}</b><br>确定要保存吗？`,
                    icon: 'warning',
                    showCancelButton: true,
                    confirmButtonColor: '#ef4444',
                    cancelButtonColor: '#3b82f6',
                    confirmButtonText: '确定保存',
                    cancelButtonText: '再检查下'
                }).then((result) => {
                    if (result.isConfirmed) {
                        billForm.submit();
                    }
                });
                return;
            }

            // 验证通过，直接提交
            billForm.submit();
        });
    });

    // 配置 marked 选项以支持表格和换行
    marked.setOptions({
        gfm: true,
        breaks: true, // 允许回车换行
        headerIds: false, // 简化渲染
        highlight: function (code, lang) {
            const language = hljs.getLanguage(lang) ? lang : 'plaintext';
            return hljs.highlight(code, {language}).value;
        }
    });

    // ------------- 新增分类按钮交互 -------------
    // 根据 data-id 填充图标（避免复杂的 Thymeleaf 三元表达式）
    function hydrateCategoryIcons() {
        const map = {
            '1': 'fa-wallet',
            '2': 'fa-gift',
            '3': 'fa-utensils',
            '4': 'fa-bus',
            '5': 'fa-shopping-bag',
            '6': 'fa-home'
        };
        document.querySelectorAll('.category-grid .cat-btn').forEach(btn => {
            const id = btn.getAttribute('data-id');
            const iconEl = btn.querySelector('.icon');
            if (iconEl) {
                // 如果按钮本身没有 data-id (例如“全部”)，跳过
                if (id && map[id]) {
                    iconEl.classList.add(map[id]);
                }
            }
        });
    }

    function selectFilterCategory(id, el) {
        // 设置隐藏的 filter input
        document.getElementById('filterCategoryId').value = id === '' ? '' : id;
        // 切换样式
        const group = document.getElementById('filterCategoryGrid');
        group.querySelectorAll('.cat-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        if (el) el.classList.add('active');
    }

    function selectAddCategory(id, el) {
        document.getElementById('addBillCategoryId').value = id;
        // 切换样式
        const group = el.closest('.category-grid');
        group.querySelectorAll('.cat-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        el.classList.add('active');
    }

    // 在添加账单表单提交前校验是否选择了分类
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        // 填充图标
        hydrateCategoryIcons();
        const addBillForm = document.querySelector('#addBillModal form');
        if (addBillForm) {
            addBillForm.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
                const v = document.getElementById('addBillCategoryId').value;
                if (!v) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    alert('请选择分类');
                    // 给第一个按钮一个焦点提示
                    const firstBtn = document.querySelector('#addBillModal .category-grid .cat-btn');
                    if (firstBtn) firstBtn.scrollIntoView({behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center'});
                    return false;
                }
            });
        }

        // 如果 filter 已由后端渲染选中，则确保前端样式与隐藏输入一致（双保险）
        const filterVal = document.getElementById('filterCategoryId') ? document.getElementById('filterCategoryId').value : '';
        if (filterVal !== null) {
            const group = document.getElementById('filterCategoryGrid');
            if (group) {
                group.querySelectorAll('.cat-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
                if (filterVal === '' || filterVal === 'null') {
                    const allBtn = group.querySelector('.cat-btn');
                    if (allBtn) allBtn.classList.add('active');
                } else {
                    // 直接通过 data-id 查找（更稳健）
                    const target = group.querySelector(".cat-btn[data-id='" + filterVal + "']");
                    if (target) target.classList.add('active');
                }
            }
        }
    });
    // ------------- 结束 分类按钮交互 -------------

    // 2. 发送消息
    window.hasAutoReloaded = false; // 防止重复刷新标志

    function sendMsg() {
        const text = chatInput.value.trim();
        if (!text) return;

        // 添加用户消息
        addMessage(text, 'user');
        chatInput.value = '';

        // 创建 Bot 消息
        const thinkingId = 'thinking-' + Date.now();
        const thinkingHtml = `
            <div class="typing-indicator" id="${thinkingId}">
                <span></span><span></span><span></span>
            </div>`;

        const botBubble = addMessage(thinkingHtml, 'bot');

        // 👇 核心逻辑1：开始思考，添加彩虹背景
        botBubble.classList.add('thinking');

        let fullText = "";
        let isFirstToken = true;

        // SSE 流式请求
        const eventSource = new EventSource(`/ai/chat?message=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`);

        // 监听自定义事件，强制刷新
        eventSource.addEventListener('magic', function (event) {
            if (!window.hasAutoReloaded) {
                window.hasAutoReloaded = true;
                const reloadMsg = document.createElement('div');
                reloadMsg.className = 'alert alert-success mt-2 mb-0 p-2 small';
                reloadMsg.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sync fa-spin me-1"></i> 系统指令：正在刷新数据...';
                botBubble.appendChild(reloadMsg);

                // 保存状态后刷新
                saveChatState();
                setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 1000);
            }
        });

        eventSource.onmessage = function (event) {
            const delta = event.data.replace(/<br_mark>/g, '\n');
            // 👇 核心修复：更严格的判定
            // 只有当 delta 包含可见字符时，才认为思考结束。
            // 这样可以忽略开头的空数据包或纯换行符，防止气泡提前塌陷。
            if (isFirstToken && (!delta || delta.trim().length === 0)) {
                return;
            }

            // 👇 核心逻辑2：收到真正文字 -> 移除动画节点 -> 移除彩虹背景
            if (isFirstToken) {
                const indicator = document.getElementById(thinkingId);
                if (indicator) indicator.remove();

                // 瞬间去掉 .thinking 类，恢复默认颜色
                botBubble.classList.remove('thinking');

                // 恢复 display 属性，以便 markdown 内容正常排版（否则 flex 会打乱表格布局）
                botBubble.style.display = 'inline-block';

                isFirstToken = false;
            }

            fullText += delta;
// 🟢 新增逻辑：智能检测是否需要刷新页面
            if (fullText.includes("系统已自动写入数据库") && !window.hasAutoReloaded) {
                window.hasAutoReloaded = true;
                setTimeout(() => {
                    const reloadMsg = document.createElement('div');
                    reloadMsg.className = 'alert alert-success mt-2 mb-0 p-2 small';
                    reloadMsg.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sync fa-spin me-1"></i> 检测到数据更新，正在刷新页面...';
                    botBubble.appendChild(reloadMsg);

                    // 保存状态后刷新
                    saveChatState();
                    setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 1500);
                }, 1000);
            }
            // 下面的逻辑保持之前的 Markdown 修复逻辑
            let fixFormatText = fullText;
            fixFormatText = fixFormatText.replace(/(^|\n)(#{1,6})([^\s#])/g, '$1$2 $3');
            fixFormatText = fixFormatText.replace(/([^\n])(\n#{1,6}\s)/g, '$1\n\n$2');

            botBubble.innerHTML = marked.parse(fixFormatText);

            // 高亮代码块
            botBubble.querySelectorAll('pre code').forEach((block) => {
                hljs.highlightElement(block);
            });
            scrollToBottom();
        };

        eventSource.onerror = function () {
            eventSource.close();
            if (isFirstToken) {
                botBubble.classList.remove('thinking');
                botBubble.style.display = 'inline-block';
                botBubble.innerHTML = "<span class='text-danger'>连接超时或网络错误 🤯</span>";
            }
        };
    }

    // 3. 上传文件
    function uploadFile() {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
        const file = fileInput.files[0];
        if (!file) return;

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append("file", file);

        // 添加一个正在上传的提示
        const loadingBubble = addMessage(`📄 正在解析文件: ${file.name}...`, 'user');

        fetch('/ai/upload', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.error) {
                    loadingBubble.innerText = "❌ 上传失败: " + data.error;
                } else {
                    loadingBubble.innerText = `📄 已上传文件 [${data.fileName}]`;
                    // 将文件内容填入输入框，让用户决定什么时候发送
                    chatInput.value = data.content;
                    chatInput.focus();
                }
            })
            .catch(err => {
                loadingBubble.innerText = "❌ 网络错误";
            });

        fileInput.value = ''; // 重置
    }

    // 4. 通用工具
    function addMessage(htmlContent, role) {
        const msgDiv = document.createElement('div');
        msgDiv.className = `message ${role}`;
        msgDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="bubble">${htmlContent}</div>`;
        chatBody.appendChild(msgDiv);
        scrollToBottom();
        return msgDiv.querySelector('.bubble');
    }

    function scrollToBottom() {
        chatBody.scrollTop = chatBody.scrollHeight;
    }

    function clearHistory() {
        fetch('/ai/clear', {method: 'POST'}).then(() => {
            chatBody.innerHTML = '<div class="message bot"><div class="bubble">记忆已清除 🧠</div></div>';
        });
    }

    // 🆕 新增：生成随机账单
    function generateBills() {
        const count = document.getElementById('genCount').value;
        if (confirm(`确定要随机生成 ${count} 笔模拟账单吗？`)) {
            fetch('/bill/generate?count=' + count, {method: 'POST'})
                .then(() => {
                    // 👇 修改提示语，解释分页情况
                    alert(`成功生成 ${count} 笔账单！\n\n提示：\n列表默认仅显示【最新10条】。\n请翻页或调整查询日期查看全部数据。`);
                    // 保存状态后刷新
                    saveChatState();
                    window.location.reload();
                });
        }
    }

    // 🆕 新增：清空所有账单
    function clearBills() {
        if (confirm('⚠️ 警告：这将清空您所有的记账数据，且不可恢复！\n确定要清空吗？')) {
            fetch('/bill/clear', {method: 'POST'})
                .then(() => {
                    alert('数据已清空。');
                    // 保存状态后刷新
                    saveChatState();
                    window.location.reload();
                });
        }
    }

    // 🆕 新增：财务健康体检
    function checkHealth() {
        const content = document.getElementById('healthContent');
        const loading = document.getElementById('healthLoading');

        content.style.display = 'none';
        loading.style.display = 'block';

        // 模拟计算延迟感
        setTimeout(() => {
            fetch('/bill/health')
                .then(res => res.json())
                .then(data => {
                    loading.style.display = 'none';
                    content.style.display = 'block';

                    let colorClass = data.type === 'danger' ? 'text-danger' : (data.type === 'success' ? 'text-success' : 'text-warning');

                    content.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="display-6 mb-2">${data.score}</div>
                    <div class="fw-bold ${colorClass} mb-2">${data.score.split(" ")[1] || data.score}</div>
                    <p class="small text-muted border-top pt-2">${data.advice}</p>
                `;
                });
        }, 800);
    }

    // 🆕 新增：计算器逻辑
    let calcExpression = "0";
    let shouldReset = false; // 计算完后是否重置

    function calcInput(val) {
        if (shouldReset && !['+', '-', '*', '/'].includes(val)) {
            calcExpression = "0";
            shouldReset = false;
        }
        if (shouldReset && ['+', '-', '*', '/'].includes(val)) {
            shouldReset = false; // 继续运算
        }

        if (calcExpression === "0" && !['+', '-', '*', '/'].includes(val)) {
            calcExpression = val;
        } else {
            calcExpression += val;
        }
        updateDisplay();
    }

    function calcClear() {
        calcExpression = "0";
        shouldReset = false;
        updateDisplay();
    }

    function calcResult() {
        try {
            // 安全性注意：eval在前端计算器场景通常可接受，但需注意
            // 这里简单处理，实际可使用 Math.js 库
            let result = eval(calcExpression);
            // 处理浮点数精度
            if (!Number.isInteger(result)) {
                result = parseFloat(result.toFixed(2));
            }
            calcExpression = result.toString();
            shouldReset = true;
            updateDisplay();
        } catch (e) {
            calcExpression = "Error";
            shouldReset = true;
            updateDisplay();
        }
    }

    function updateDisplay() {
        const display = document.getElementById('calcDisplay');
        display.innerText = calcExpression;
        // 自动滚动到最右侧
        display.scrollLeft = display.scrollWidth;
    }

    // 🆕 新增：预算设置逻辑
    let budgetModalInstance = null;

    function getBudgetModal() {
        if (!budgetModalInstance) {
            budgetModalInstance = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('budgetModal'));
        }
        return budgetModalInstance;
    }

    function openBudgetModal() {
        // 默认显示当前月
        const now = new Date();
        const monthStr = now.toISOString().slice(0, 7); // yyyy-MM
        document.getElementById('budgetMonth').value = monthStr;
        document.getElementById('budgetAmount').value = '';
        getBudgetModal().show();
    }

    function saveBudget() {
        const month = document.getElementById('budgetMonth').value;
        const amount = document.getElementById('budgetAmount').value;

        if (!month || !amount) {
            alert("请填写完整信息");
            return;
        }

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append("month", month);
        formData.append("amount", amount);

        fetch('/budget/ajax/save', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: formData
        })
            .then(res => res.text())
            .then(txt => {
                if (txt === 'ok') {
                    alert("预算设置成功！");
                    getBudgetModal().hide();
                    // 刷新页面以显示最新预算信息
                    window.location.reload();
                } else {
                    alert("设置失败：" + txt);
                }
            })
            .catch(err => {
                alert("网络错误：" + err);
            });
    }
</script>

<div class="login-card text-center">
    <h3 class="mb-4 fw-bold" style="color: #667eea;">
        <i class="fas fa-wallet me-2"></i>MyBookkeeping
    </h3>
    <form action="/login" class="text-start" method="post">
        <div class="mb-3">
            <label class="form-label text-secondary">账号</label>
            <div class="input-group">
                <span class="input-group-text bg-light border-end-0"><i class="fas fa-user text-muted"></i></span>

                <span class="input-group-text bg-light border-end-0"><i class="fas fa-lock text-muted"></i></span>
                <input class="form-control border-start-0 ps-0" name="password" placeholder="请输入密码" required
                       type="password">

<!-- 2. 动态生成的 Hanabi 舞台 -->
<div id="hanabi-stage"></div>

<!-- JS 逻辑 -->
<script th:inline="javascript">
    const stage = document.getElementById('hanabi-stage');
    const imgPath = /*[[@{/images/hanabi.jpg}]]*/ '/images/hanabi.jpg';
    const colors = ['#FF6B6B', '#4ECDC4', '#FFE66D', '#FF9F43', '#5f27cd'];
    // 对应 CSS 中的新动画类名
    const animations = ['anim-pulse', 'anim-swing', 'anim-spin', 'anim-shake', 'anim-bob'];
    const messages = ['✨ 点我帮你分析', '💰 记得记账哦', '🤖 AI理财助手', '📈 拒绝月光!', '🎉 欢迎回来!'];

    // 存储所有漂浮对象的状态
    const floaters = [];
    const speedBase = 0.8; // 基础移动速度

    // 生成5个
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        createHanabi(i);
    }

    // 启动动画循环
    requestAnimationFrame(updatePositions);

    function createHanabi(index) {
        const el = document.createElement('div');
        el.className = 'hanabi-item';

        // 1. 构建 HTML 内容
        const color = colors[index % colors.length];
        const animClass = animations[index % animations.length];
        const msg = messages[index % messages.length];

        let htmlInner = `<div class="hanabi-bubble" style="color: ${color}; border-color: ${color}">${msg}</div>`;
        htmlInner += `<img src="${imgPath}" class="hanabi-avatar ${animClass}" style="border-color: ${color}; color: ${color};">`;

        el.innerHTML = htmlInner;

        // 2. 先添加到舞台，以便计算真实尺寸
        stage.appendChild(el);

        // 3. 计算尺寸和随机属性 (关键步骤)
        const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        // 随机大小 (1.0 ~ 1.5倍)
        const scale = 1.0 + Math.random() * 0.5;

        // 计算缩放后的实际占用宽高，用于碰撞检测
        const realWidth = rect.width * scale;
        const realHeight = rect.height * scale;

        // 4. 随机初始位置 (确保完全在屏幕内)
        // 减去 realWidth/realHeight 确保生成时不会只有一般在屏幕内
        const maxX = window.innerWidth - realWidth;
        const maxY = window.innerHeight - realHeight;

        // Math.max(0, ...) 防止窗口极小导致负数
        let x = Math.random() * Math.max(0, maxX);
        let y = Math.random() * Math.max(0, maxY);

        // 5. 随机速度向量 (vx, vy)
        let vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * speedBase * 2;
        let vy = (Math.random() - 0.5) * speedBase * 2;

        // 存入数组
        floaters.push({
            el: el,
            x: x,
            y: y,
            vx: vx,
            vy: vy,
            scale: scale,
            width: realWidth,
            height: realHeight
        });
    }

    function updatePositions() {
        const maxX = window.innerWidth;
        const maxY = window.innerHeight;

        floaters.forEach(obj => {
            // 更新位置
            obj.x += obj.vx;
            obj.y += obj.vy;

            // 边界碰撞检测 (反弹逻辑)
            // 左边界 或 右边界 (x + 宽度 >= 屏幕宽)
            if (obj.x <= 0) {
                obj.x = 0; // 修正位置，防止卡在墙里
                obj.vx *= -1; // 速度反向
            } else if (obj.x + obj.width >= maxX) {
                obj.x = maxX - obj.width;
                obj.vx *= -1;
            }

            // 上边界 或 下边界 (y + 高度 >= 屏幕高)
            if (obj.y <= 0) {
                obj.y = 0;
                obj.vy *= -1;
            } else if (obj.y + obj.height >= maxY) {
                obj.y = maxY - obj.height;
                obj.vy *= -1;
            }

            // 应用样式
            // 使用 translate3d 开启硬件加速，移动更流畅
            obj.el.style.transform = `translate3d(${obj.x}px, ${obj.y}px, 0) scale(${obj.scale})`;
        });

        requestAnimationFrame(updatePositions);
    }
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script th:inline="javascript">
    // 将 Thymeleaf 表达式放在 JS 注释占位符中，并提供空数组作为回退值
    const treeData = /*[[${treeData}]]*/ [];

    // 调试输出，查看渲染后的内容（在浏览器控制台查看）
    console.log('treemap treeData:', treeData);

    // 兼容处理：后端有时会传单个对象而不是数组
    let preparedData = treeData;
    if (treeData && !Array.isArray(treeData)) {
        preparedData = [treeData];
    }

    const noDataEl = document.getElementById('noData');
    const chartEl = document.getElementById('treeChart');

    // 若没有有效数据，显示提示并退出
    if (!preparedData || !Array.isArray(preparedData) || preparedData.length === 0) {
        if (noDataEl) noDataEl.style.display = 'block';
    } else {
        if (noDataEl) noDataEl.style.display = 'none';
        try {
            const chart = echarts.init(chartEl);

            chart.setOption({
                tooltip: {
                    trigger: 'item',
                    triggerOn: 'mousemove',
                    formatter: function (params) {
                        if (params.data && params.data.value) {
                            return `${params.name}`;
                        }
                        return params.name;
                    }
                },
                series: [
                    {
                        type: 'tree',
                        data: preparedData,
                        top: '1%',
                        left: '7%',
                        bottom: '1%',
                        right: '25%',
                        symbolSize: 12,
                        label: {
                            position: 'left',
                            verticalAlign: 'middle',
                            align: 'right',
                            fontSize: 14
                        },
                        leaves: {
                            label: {
                                position: 'right',
                                verticalAlign: 'middle',
                                align: 'left'
                            }
                        },
                        emphasis: {
                            focus: 'descendant'
                        },
                        expandAndCollapse: true,
                        animationDuration: 550,
                        animationDurationUpdate: 750
                    }
                ]
            });

            window.addEventListener('resize', () => chart.resize());
        } catch (e) {
            console.error('ECharts 初始化/渲染出错：', e);
            if (noDataEl) {
                noDataEl.style.display = 'block';
                noDataEl.textContent = '图表渲染失败，请查看控制台错误信息';
            }
        }
    }
</script>

========== FILE: E:\LEAR-CODE\Accounting system\src\main\resources\schema.sql ==========

-- ===================================================================
-- 🟢 自动初始化脚本
-- 当项目启动时，Spring Boot 会自动检测并执行此文件。
-- 作用：如果对方数据库里没有表，这里会自动创建，确保新环境能直接运行！
-- ===================================================================

-- 1. 用户表 (用于登录和注册)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user (
  id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  username VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  email VARCHAR(100),
  created_time DATETIME
);

-- 2. 分类表
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS category (
  id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT,
  name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  type INT NOT NULL COMMENT '1:收入 2:支出'
);

-- 预置一些分类数据 (使用 INSERT IGNORE 避免重复报错)
-- 这里的 user_id = 0 代表系统公共分类
INSERT IGNORE INTO category (id, user_id, name, type) VALUES 
(1, 0, '工资', 1), 
(2, 0, '奖金', 1), 
(3, 0, '餐饮', 2), 
(4, 0, '交通', 2), 
(5, 0, '购物', 2), 
(6, 0, '住房', 2);

-- 3. 账单表
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bill (
  id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
  amount DECIMAL(10,2) NOT NULL,
  type INT NOT NULL,
  category_id BIGINT,
  bill_date DATE,
  remark VARCHAR(255)
);

-- 4. 预算表
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS budget (
  id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
  month VARCHAR(7),
  total_budget DECIMAL(10,2),
  used_amount DECIMAL(10,2)
);

-- 5. 财务目标表
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS financial_goal (
  id BIGINT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
  target_amount DECIMAL(10,2),
  target_date DATE,
  created_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);
